# Skills · R · part 137

916 entries, reticle-verify to retro. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **reticle-verify**: Drive the running app with Reticle and turn the result into Done evidence. Use after any UI, route or copy change in a repo that has `.reticle.json` (CARSI today), before calling the work done, before the PR release gate, or when asked "does it actually work in the browser" / "verify the flow" / "reticle". Combines the vercel:verification method (story, evidence, data flow, report) with Reticle's pass/fail verdicts, and gives donectl a command whose exit code is the proof. ([CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops](https://github.com/CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops/tree/HEAD/skills-library/skills/reticle-verify) · ★1)
- **reticle-verify**: Use Reticle to prove whether a change works in a real running web or desktop app. Trigger when asked to verify a coding agent's claimed fix, investigate a UI bug, validate a user-facing change, replay a saved journey, or distinguish a real pass from an unknown result. ([laxmi-narayan-87/plugins](https://github.com/laxmi-narayan-87/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/reticle-verification/skills/reticle-verify))
- **reticle-verify-ui-change**: Check that a change to a web app actually works in the running app before calling it done. Drives the real page and returns a pass/fail verdict with the request that fired, the state that moved, and the file:line to fix. Use after editing a component, a form, a route, or an API call; when you have said "fixed" but have not opened the app; when the user asks "does it actually work?"; or when a change looks right on screen and you cannot prove it. ([prashantdevelopment/Cortex](https://github.com/prashantdevelopment/Cortex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reticle-verify-ui-change) · 2 copies)

Reviews the trail since the last retro (check-ins, decisions, what got done), traces movement on each goal, names repeating patterns, and proposes exactly ONE concrete improvement adoptable this week. The compounding engine: a friction seen twice escalates into a goal or a growth edge in identity.md, so the system accrues self-knowledge instead of restarting. Honest mirror — no spin. Triggers: /retro, "run a retro", "weekly retro", "retrospective", "look back at the week", "am I getting better".
